# Kotodama Compiler Progress Archive

This is the verbatim historical `ROADMAP.md` Active Task Notes table covering 2026-04-21 through 2026-05-31. It is retained for audit history; current work belongs in the main roadmap table.

| ID | Date | Progress |
| --- | --- | --- |
| EX-207 | 2026-04-21 | Added pointer-typed scalar state read/write lowering for `AccountId`, `AssetDefinitionId`, `DomainId`, `Name`, `Blob`, and `NoritoBytes`; added the current subscription and ZK syscall surface; accepted current upstream method-call forms (`map.contains`, `map.ensure`); supported `state Map<K, V>` helper parameters; and matched current no-argument public/view entrypoint lowering. Refreshed successful upstream `threshold_escrow.ko` and `zk_vote_and_unshield.ko` samples compile in the browser, while subscription samples now match Rust failure kind/diagnostics when public functions use privileged operations without `permission(...)`. Remaining gaps: inline ZK builder payload byte parity, broader exact artifact/manifest/source-map parity across the corpus, fuller semantic analysis, and non-inlined ABI parity. |
| EX-221 | 2026-04-21 | Strict parity refresh now writes every discovered upstream fixture, including Rust compile failures, and `KOTO_BIN` mode runs from the upstream Iroha root so relative source paths resolve consistently. The checked-in corpus now contains 22 upstream fixtures plus 1 local negative fixture, with 2 upstream failures preserved as fixtures; parity tests cover full-corpus success/failure kind parity, normalized failure diagnostics, and current direct-entrypoint metadata shape. Exact byte-for-byte parity remains scoped to earlier locked fixtures and is still open for the broader refreshed corpus. |
| EX-207 | 2026-04-21 | Follow-up slice: direct static `transfer_asset`, `mint_asset`, and `burn_asset` calls now derive bounded asset/account/asset-definition access keys when all host-call targets are compile-time literals or literal constructors, instead of collapsing to wildcard host effects. Entrypoint access keys now preserve semantic discovery order while aggregate `access_set_hints` remain sorted, matching Rust's split behavior for the `ivm_smoke.ko` static-transfer fixture. This closes the refreshed corpus aggregate access-set gap while leaving bytecode/frame layout, helper-call ABI, and exact artifact parity open. |
| EX-221 | 2026-04-21 | Added focused parity coverage for the `ivm-smoke` aggregate access-set match.
